Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the ecosystems framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 369. The resulting operator achieves bound O(945/n^3).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to ecosystems structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 369/945 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the ecosystems structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 376. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 207 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 376 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the ecosystems structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 207. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
